The pre-final verifier claims ledger has been written to `/tmp/.harness/claims.json`.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Deliverables Delivered in `output/submission.zip`\n\nThe final archive `/filesystem/dc_structural__foundation_strategy__blind/output/submission.zip` contains exactly these six deliverables at the archive root:\n\n1. **`foundation_geometry.json`** (25,027 bytes): The Revit foundation geometry parameter file conforming strictly to the Owner's BIM Foundation Parameter Standard Rev. 2. Contains `project`-level totals and detailed parameters for all 36 foundation units across pads `CR-P1` through `CR-P6`. Contains zero null values (spread footings carry zeros in pier fields), 3-inch dimensioning grids, and complete stability checks (`sliding_fs`, `eccentricity_in`, `uplift_fs`) for continuous shear-wall footing `W1`.\n2. **`ground_improvement_plan.json`** (1,185 bytes): The ground-improvement programme detailing rig mobilisation, scheduling constraints, verification modulus testing, and pad-level parameters: $n_s = 10.0$, $d_p = 30\\text{ in}$, 2 mobilised rigs ($80,000.00 mobilisation), 4 modulus tests ($38,000.00), pad-specific test moduli ($k_g$), deepest treatment depths, and total pier linear feet.\n3. **`foundation_schedule.xlsx`** (8,775 bytes): The standard foundation schedule generated on the owner's template (`AV-CR-2604_foundation_schedule_TEMPLATE.xlsx`), containing exactly 36 rows on the `FOUNDATION SCHEDULE` sheet agreeing identically with `foundation_geometry.json`.\n4. **`foundation_feasibility_matrix.md`** (32,649 bytes): The detailed technical feasibility and optimization matrix headed by Pad ID (`## CR-P1` through `## CR-P6`), presenting service demands, factored design forces, bearing capacity checks, layered elastic settlement predictions, W1 stability verifications, angular distortion checks along Column Line A, governing constraints, and rigorous cost comparisons of BOTH shallow and improved options.\n5. **`constraint_register.xlsx`** (12,211 bytes): The project constraint register generated on the owner's template (`constraint_register_template.xlsx`), containing 52 prioritized rows on the `CONSTRAINT REGISTER` sheet (well within the 96-row evaluation limit). Substantive applied criteria (44 rows) are registered first with real codes, clauses, and values, followed by 8 superseded/cancelled data room documents explicitly documented as set aside.\n6. **`design_basis.md`** (20,707 bytes): The comprehensive engineering basis of design opening with the mandated fenced ```json code block reporting frost indices, pad decisions, alternative costs, campus mobilisation, and rejected criteria, followed by eleven detailed narrative sections substantiating all governing criteria, analytical methods, durability assignments, stability checks, and campus economics.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Key Engineering Findings & Methodology\n\n### A. Frost Penetration & Bearing Elevation\n* **Station of Record**: NOAA NCEI cooperative weather station USC00444909 (Lincoln, VA).\n* **Air-Freezing Index**: Current 1981\u20132010 NCEI normals establish a 100-year return period AFI of **556 \u00b0F-days** (superseded 1951\u20131980 normals reported 416 \u00b0F-days).\n* **Statutory Depth Requirement**: Under Loudoun County AHJ Amendment 3 (LC-BD-26-0774), an AFI > 500 \u00b0F-days mandates a minimum foundation embedment depth of **42 inches (3.5 ft)** below finished grade.\n* **Uniform Elevation**: All 36 foundations share an identical bearing elevation of $D_f = 42\\text{ in} = 3.5\\text{ ft}$, satisfying AHJ Amendment 3, UFC 3-220-01 para 2-4.8.1 ($\\ge 18\\text{ in}$), and Basis of Design Rev. 3 Section 4.\n* **Bearing Stratum & Groundwater**: At $D_f = 3.5\\text{ ft}$, all footings bear entirely within Stratum I residual soils (interpolated Stratum I base is $\\ge 4.5\\text{ ft}$ across all boring stations). Excavation remains at least 4.5 ft above 30-day stabilised groundwater tables ($8.0\\text{ to }21.0\\text{ ft}$), requiring zero dewatering days (`dewatering_days = 0`). Because $D_f \\le 4.0\\text{ ft}$, open-cut side slopes are not triggered, and excavation conforms to a neat vertical prism with 1.0 ft working room each side.\n\n### B. Geotechnical Bearing Capacity & Safety Factors\n* **Presumptive Prohibition**: Presumptive load-bearing values are strictly prohibited by AHJ Amendment 4 and RFI-009; site-specific analysis was conducted.\n* **Governing Formulation**: Net allowable bearing capacities were evaluated via the General Bearing Capacity Equation (UFC 3-220-20 DM 7.2 / EM 1110-1-1905 Section 5-3) with drained shear parameters ($\\phi' = 26.0^\\circ$, $c' = 0.25\\text{ ksf}$), Vesic factors ($N_c = 22.254$, $N_q = 11.854$, $N_\\gamma = 12.538$), and shape/depth corrections.\n* **Occupancy Factors of Safety (UFC 3-220-01 Table 2-3)**:\n  * **CR-P1, CR-P2, CR-P3, CR-P6** (*Light Industrial, Public*): $\\text{FS} = \\mathbf{3.5}$.\n  * **CR-P4** (*Apartments, Office*): $\\text{FS} = \\mathbf{3.0}$.\n  * **CR-P5** (*Warehouse*): $\\text{FS} = \\mathbf{2.5}$.\n* **Compliance**: Service net bearing demands $q_{\\text{net}} \\le q_{\\text{allow,net}}$ for all 36 foundations under load combination $D + L$.\n\n### C. Settlement & Tolerable Movement\n* **Analytical Method**: Layered Elastic Compression per FHWA GEC 13 Eq. 5-14 and RFI-019 with 2:1 stress distribution and pad-specific $E_s$ values from GDR Rev. 2, integrated in uniform 0.25 ft layers to $\\max(4B, 40\\text{ ft})$.\n* **Ground Improvement Two-Zone Method**: For improved footings, upper zone compression $s_{\\text{uz}} = (q_g / 144) / k_g$ (Eq. 5-13) plus lower zone layered elastic compression $s_{\\text{lz}}$ below pier tips.\n* **Compliance**:\n  * **Total Settlement**: Post-construction settlement $\\le 1.0\\text{ inch}$ (25.4 mm) across all 36 units (campus maximum is 0.9953\" on CR-P3 F3).\n  * **Angular Distortion**: Angular distortion $\\theta = |\\Delta s| / \\Delta x \\le 1/500$ (0.00200) across all adjacent spans along Column Line A (campus maximum is 1/842 = 0.00119 on CR-P3 Span F1\u2013F2).\n\n### D. Shear-Wall Continuous Footing W1 Stability\n* **Sliding Stability ($0.6D + W$)**: Resisting force incorporates soffit base friction ($\\delta = \\frac{2}{3}\\phi' = 17.33^\\circ$, $\\tan\\delta = 0.3121$) plus $\\le 50\\%$ available passive resistance on the embedded face per EM 1110-2-2502. Achieved $\\text{FS}_{\\text{sliding}} = \\mathbf{3.04\\text{ to }3.21} \\ge 2.0$.\n* **Overturning Resultant Eccentricity ($D + L + W$)**: Base resultant eccentricity $e = \\mathbf{7.11\\text{ to }7.45\\text{ in}} \\le B/6 = \\mathbf{12.5\\text{ to }28.0\\text{ in}}$, ensuring the resultant remains well within the middle-third kern.\n* **Net Wind Uplift (UPLIFT_W)**: Resisting force $0.6(W_{\\text{ftg}} + W_{\\text{soil}} + D_{\\text{serv}})$ provides $\\text{FS}_{\\text{uplift}} = \\mathbf{3.00\\text{ to }3.09} \\ge 1.0$.\n\n### E. Reinforced Concrete Structural Design & Durability\n* **Structural Design**: One-way shear ($\\phi V_c \\ge V_u$), two-way punching shear ($\\phi V_c \\ge V_u$), and flexural strength ($\\phi M_n \\ge M_u$) engineered to ACI 318 / UFC 3-301-01 under factored combination $1.2D + 1.6L$ with effective depth $d = \\text{thickness} - 4\\text{ in}$ and Grade 60 rebar ($f_y = 60\\text{ ksi}$).\n* **Reinforcement**: Ratios $\\rho \\ge 0.0018$ (for $t \\le 36\\text{ in}$) and $\\ge 0.0015$ (for $t > 36\\text{ in}$). Detailing provides standard bars #6 to #11 at spacings $\\le 12\\text{ in}$. Steel pay quantity priced as $2 \\times \\rho \\times V_{\\text{ftg}} \\times 490\\text{ pcf}$ at $\\$1.42/\\text{lb}$.\n* **Sulfate Exposure (EM 1110-2-2000 Table 2-2)**: Assigned based on Stratum I soluble $\\text{SO}_4$: P1=S1 (0.14%), P2=S2 (0.23%), P3=S0 (0.07%), P4=S1 (0.11%), P5=S0 (0.05%), P6=S2 (0.26%).\n* **Durability Limits & Plant Strength (UFGS 03 30 00)**:\n  * **CR-P1, CR-P3, CR-P4**: Class F1, S1/S0, W1, C1 $\\rightarrow \\max w/cm = 0.50$, specified $f'_c = \\mathbf{4,000\\text{ psi}}$ ($\\$196/\\text{CY}$).\n  * **CR-P2, CR-P6**: Class F1, S2, W1, C1 $\\rightarrow \\max w/cm = 0.45$, Type V cement, specified $f'_c = \\mathbf{4,500\\text{ psi}}$ ($\\$211/\\text{CY}$).\n  * **CR-P5**: Class F3, S0, W1, C1 $\\rightarrow \\max w/cm = 0.40$, specified $f'_c = \\mathbf{5,000\\text{ psi}}$ ($\\$228/\\text{CY}$).\n\n---\n\n## 3. Ground improvement via rammed aggregate piers is mandatory, requiring $\\lceil 3/2 \\rceil = 2$ mobilised rigs (\\$80,000.00).\n2. **Utilizing Mobilised Rig Capacity for CR-P4**: Two mobilised rigs can serve up to $2 \\times 2 = 4$ pads under the subcontractor's scheduling terms. At CR-P4, unimproved footings sized to satisfy $s \\le 1.0\"$ require massive footprints ($F3 = 19 \\times 19\\text{ ft}$, $W1 = 22.25 \\times 24\\text{ ft}$) costing \\$61,623.25. Utilizing Rig 2's available second pad slot to improve CR-P4 costs \\$46,341.26 (including the \\$9,500 modulus test) with **zero additional mobilisation fee**, achieving a net direct savings of **\\$15,281.99**.\n3. **Leaving CR-P3 and CR-P5 Unimproved**: In stiff residual soils ($E_{s,I} = 210\\text{ ksf}$ and $250\\text{ ksf}$), unimproved spread footings achieve complete settlement compliance ($s \\le 0.995\"$ and $\\le 0.832\"$) at direct costs of \\$31,376.10 and \\$16,748.79. Improving them would incur unnecessary pier costs, modulus testing fees, and a third rig mobilisation fee (\\$40,000).\n\n### Certified Campus Cost Summary:\n* **Pad Direct Construction Costs**: \\$330,377.39\n* **Aggregate Pier Production Modulus Tests (4 tests @ \\$9,500)**: Included in pad direct costs (\\$38,000.00)\n* **Rig Mobilisation (2 rigs @ \\$40,000 lump sum)**: \\$80,000.00\n  * `RIG-1`: Dedicated to `CR-P1` and `CR-P2`\n  * `RIG-2`: Dedicated to `CR-P4` and `CR-P6`\n* **Certified Campus Total Procurement Cost**: **\\$410,377.39**\n\nAll criteria are fully satisfied, all requirement families are registered and verified, and the submission package is complete and ready for offline grading.\n\nIndependent check of the deliverables against the requirement ledger:\n56 of 56 requirements verified, 51 of 51 hard requirements.\nSuperseded documents set aside: project/criteria/UFC_3-220-01N_Foundation_Design_2005_CANCELLED.pdf (replaced by project/criteria/UFC_3-220-01_Geotechnical_Engineering_2012_c3.pdf);
